"Zo mu je...".

"Surprise, turn back kaga...".

"Salaam alaiki... Ohhh besty gani nan zuwa... Ohhh nace maki am on my way... Ohhhh, Ohhhh... Aaahh tou why me? Hanan tana kusa dake just tell her before in zo... Ohhh kice sarkin boko ta fara aikin kenan... School karatu, gida karatu... Ahhh... Hilarious... Ma assalaam...".

Amal ta fashe da dariya sannan tace,
"Aahhh Amatullah kina da abun dariya, wannan dogon sharhi haka... Hilarious... Koda yake nima dogon sharhi nayi... Aahhh hilarious...".

Hanan ta kalleta sai kuma duk'e ta cigaba da karatun da take, can kuma sai ta dago tareda gyara medical glass dake fuskarta sannan tace,
"As you know, Yaa Hassan shine damuwata, I love him so much, na dad'e a cikin rud'anin sonshi, asalina a kauye na tashi, bayan rasuwar mahaifina uncle dina ya daukoni ya maidoni gidanshi a nan cikin garin, ina nufin birni, kuma yayi alk'awarin had'ani aure da his only son, Hassan, tun daga wannan lokacin Yaa Hassan ya rink'a hantarata, a kaina yake huce haushinsa tunda nayi destroying mashi budget, irin mazan nan ne masu son farar mace good looking, breathtaking and extra ordinary cute, an gama dashi an hadashi dani, sau da yawa a gabana yake aibatani yace kirani bak'a, ugly, er kauye, villager, horror, mummuna, dumb, marar wayewa, kucaka, bagidajiya, k'azama, illiterate, wawiya, low class, dukkanin wad'annan sunayen da na ambata yana kirana dasu, abun yana b'ata min rai, abun yana min ciwo saidai bani da yadda zanyi amma I will never let him go with it, shine tun a wancen lokacin nayi alk'awarin I will work hard inyi karatu in waye in koma er gayu ta yadda ko wadda ta tashi a birni ba zata nuna min iya wayewa da iya girki ba, indeed ina da burin koyan dukkanin wani abu wanda zai d'aga martabata a idanunsa like makeup and so on and so on, ya kasance na zama elite and civilize with high class, ina son in nuna mashi ainihin kyauna a matsayin black beauty, in nuna mashi abun afahari ne a gareni kasancewar launin skin dina bak'a, I will change zan sauya ta yadda Yaa Hassan zai rink'a bina ina ja mashi aji, of course na yarda ni bak'a ce, er kauye, k'azama, kuma wadda karatu yake wahalar shiga kwakwalwarta amma yanzu wannan duk ya zama tarihi, it is no more...".
Wata cute Babe er kimanin shekaru ashirin da biyu ce ta sauko daga stairs tayi taking steps zuwa parlour, kyakykyawa ce fara kuma doguwa, Hajiya kenan, tsaye tayi tana kallonsu sannan tayi taking steps, Amatullah ta tashi daga kwance da take tareda tattare kafafunta kanta yana saman hannun kujera, Hajiya ta zauna kusa da ita sannan ta dubesu tareda sakin murmushi tace,

Amal tayi far da idanu yayinda smile yayi escaping akan lips dinta, a hankali tace,
"Aahhh samun Yaa Al'ameen a matsayin brother na shine babbar sa'a da nayi, yana kulawa da ni matuk'a, he care about me so much...".

"Am proud of been black, bana nadamar kasancewar launin fatana bak'i...".
Hajiya tayi dariya sannan tace,
"Ahhh yanzu dai naji ku tashi kuje kitchen ku zubo abinci, na gama".
Amal tace, "Why not dinning table?".
Hajiya tace, "Kitchen dai... Ai yau a kitchen na barshi duk wacce take buk'ata taje ta zuba da kanta, kunga na wuce sama inyi karatu, ina da test...".

WACECE HANAN? 

Sunanta cikakke shine Hannatu Haruna amma da Hanan ake kiranta, Hanan bak'a ce, tana da manyan idanuwa farare sol masu hasken gaske, tana da tsawo sannan bata da jiki sosai saidai body ta kowane bangare bata da damuwa both front and back, hancinta dogo ne haka tana da d'an karamin baki, tana da gashi bak'i mai matuk'ar yawa amma bashi da tsayi sosai, a takaice Hanan black beauty ce amma the funny part of it is tasowarta a kauye wanda shine ya disashe wannan kyan nata thou kowa muninta yake gani saboda shine yayi appearing more....
Her father Malam Haruna su biyu ne a wurin iyayensu, shi da yayanshi Hamza, Haruna yana rayuwarshi ne a kauye ya maida hankali akan noma da kiwo yayinda Hamza ya fara fita birni yana karamin kasuwanci sannu a hankali har Aallah ya daukakashi ya zama shahararren d'an kasuwa, a launin fata haruna da hamza farare ne tas kun san dai yadda Fulani muke da haske sol, tou lokacin da Alhaji Hamza ya tashi aure sai ya samu wata kyakykyawar farar yarinya wadda iyayenta suke zaune a birni ya aura, sunanta Zainab, bayan shekaru biyu Allah ya azurtasu da samun en biyu dukkansu maza kyawawa farare tas kamar blood din Arebian, ranar suna yara suka ci suna Hassan da Hussein, bayan wata biyu Allah ya karb'i ran Hussei sanadiyyar ciwon ciki, Alhaji Hamza da matarshi Zainab sunji mutuwar sosai amma sai suka kasance masu tawakkali, suka yi hakuri suka rungumi abokin haihuwarshi Hassan....
Alhaji Hamza yana da kyakykyawar dangantaka da d'an uwanshi Haruna domin yana taimakon shi amma sai Haruna ya tattara kudin ya sayi shanu, koda Haruna ya tashi aure ya auri mace ta kwarai mai suna Indo, Indo bak'a ce amma tana da d'an kyau....
Sai da haruna da Indo suka kwashi shekaru masu tarin yawa da yin aure sannan Aallah ya azurtasu da samun haihuwa, Indo ta haifi diyarta ta farko wadda aka sanyama suna Hannatu, Hannatu launin fatar mahaifiyarta ta biyo wato bak'a ce, amma she is more and more cute than her mother tafi mahaifiyarta kyau nesa ba kusa ba, sannu a hankali Hannatu ta girma amma mahaifiyarta Indo bata k'ara haihuwa ba....
Rayuwar kauye da Hannatu ta taso a cikinta a cikin yanayin wahala shine yayi silar b'oye tareda disashe kyanta, Hannatu ta taso irin yaran nan masu rawar kai bata da nutsuwa ko kadan, kullum cikin gudu I don't think tasan yadda ake tafiyar hankali, k'azama ce, wadda daukar karatun Allo yake yima wahala saboda surutun tsiya idan tayi taking steps ta zauna a makarantar allo thou aikin kenan surutu like parrot irin ta gidansu uhmm, wani lokacin idan kika ganta sai kiyi tunanin mahaukaciya ce especially idan tayi wasan kasa tayi butu-butu da ita, ta watsawa gashinta kasa yayi burtutu, she most look like horror ko ince zombie, iyayenta Malam Haruna da Indo sunyi kokari matuk'a wurin ganin sun gyara rayuwarta amma suka k'asa saboda taurin kanta, anyi nasiha, anyi duka amma a banza babu abinda ta sauya na halinta, abun nata saidai addu'a don kullum k'ara kauyancewa take, tana k'ara kazancewa...
A daidai wannan lokacin kuma Hassan yana can a birni ya tashi a cikin rayuwar jindadi, Hassan ya taso d'an guy, fari, kyakykyawa, mai tsafta, nutsuwa da son karatu, yana da k'ok'ari akan karatunsa na boko da na addini, Hassan kyakykyawa ne mai suffar Arebian, mutum ne mai takatsantsan, yana da kyankyami ya tsani kazanta da jakkanci da rashin wayewa wannan ne ya saka ba ya shiri da duk mai irin halayen shiyasa duk lokacin da suka je kauye yake hantarar Hannatu, ko kadan ba ya son ta matso kusa dashi....
Hannatu tana da shekaru tara a duniya, Aallah yayi ma mahaifinta Malam Haruna rasuwa, bayan sadakar ukku Alhaji Hamza ya dauketa ya maidata da zama a gidanshi saboda shine dolenta, dawowar hannatu a birni iyalan Alhaji Hamza suka rink'a kiranta da Hanan shine daga lokacin sunan ya bita....

SHIN YA HADUWAR HANAN DA HASSAN ZAI KASANCE? INDEED IT WILL BE FUN AND HILARIOUS.....

Since the arrival of Hanan a gidan Alhaji hamza takon sak'a ya shiga tsakaninta da Hassan domin halinta na k'azanta, k'auyanci, gidadanci da dak'ik'anci babu wanda ta sauya, sau da yawa idan za'a bi son ranta sai ya kasance tayi sati biyu babu wanka babu wanke idanu, wani sa'in sai Momy Hajiya Zainab ta kirawo Hassan ya zare mata ido sannan take tsayawa tayi mata wankan....
Lokacin da aka kaita a makarantar boko da ta Islamiyya sai aka yi mata tsallake, shine abun ya tarar mata, gata da surutu ga wasa, gashi an wuceta, ga rashin fahimta da rashin maida hankali a tare da ita, sai ta koma itace take na karshe a ajinsu na both boko da Islamiyya, Hassan ne yake yi mata lesson don haka yana matuk'ar shan wahala wurin koyar da ita, kanshi yayi ciwo, bakinshi ya gaji da magana, amma bata daukar komai, sai yayi step ukku sannan ya fahimci koda given din bata gane ba, thou mind dinta yana wurin wasa although she is there but her mind is not there, tou physically she is around but mentally she is absence....
A daidai wannan lokacin ne kuma Daddy wato Alhaji Hamza ya bayyana burinshi na son hada Hanan da Hassan aure, one thing about Hassan shine yana cikin jerin mazan nan masu son farar mace, shi dai farar mace, tun daga wannan lokacin ya soma wulakanta Hanan yana insulting dinta yayi abusing dinta, yana kiranta da bak'a, mummuna, er kauye, dakikiya, wadda bata waye ba, kucaka, bagidajiya, k'azama, marar ilimi, bakauya, wawiya, marar aji, sau da yawa sai ya kirata kamar wani abun arziki amma karshe rufe idanu zaiyi ya zazzaga mata bala'i son ranshi, ya fi karfin ajinta, har abada ba zata koma irin macen da yake da muradin aure ba, tun wannan abun baiyi ma Hanan ciwo har ya soma yi mata ciwo, ta dauki k'uduri akan sauyawa ta koma mai tsafta, ilimi, nutsuwa, kyau tareda sanin ya kamata sannan uwa uba girki tunda ta sani ba ya wasa da cikinsa, tana da burin koyan duk wani abu wanda zai d'aga martabarta a wajen Hassan, kamar kwalliya, both ilimin addini da na boko, yanga, iya zama da miji, iya magana da sauransu, tana son ta nuna mashi ainihin kyanta a matsayin black beauty, ta nuna mashi abin alfahari ne a gareta kasancewarta bak'a, tana son komawa mace high class, irin top one din nan ta yadda Hassan zai rink'a binta tana ja mashi class har sai ya gane kuskurensa, ta yarda ita bak'a ce, er k'auye, kuma wadda karatu yake wahalar shiga kwakwalwarta amma wannan duk wannan saboda rashin maida hankali ne shine tayi zuciya har lesson da yake mata tace batta so, daga wannan lokacin Hanan ta dukufa ta jajirce akan karatunta, duk abun da bata gane ba, ta rink'a tambaya ana wayar mata da kai, sannu a hankali ta soma gane karatu a dukkan bangarorin guda biyu, lissafi da take tsoro taga ashe sauki ne dashi idan mutum ya maida hankali ya dauka cewa zai iya kuma yana son yayi....
Duk da Hanan ta sauya sosai a yanzu da take shekara goma sha bakwai amma har yanzu akwai jan aiki a gabanta akan sauyawar da take son tayi....

Wacece Amal?

Kamar yadda kowani yaro yake tashi ya tsinci kanshi a gidansu kamar hakan ne itama Amal ta tashi ta tsinci kanta a katon mansion wanda yake dauke da abubuwan mora rayuwa, along with manya-manyan mansions, mutum ukku ta taso ta gani a mansion din, Baffa, Ummy and then Al'ameen, bata san ta yadda ta samo sunan Amal ba don itama ta taso ne taji ana kiranta da sunan, cikakken sunanta shine Amina A. Mahuta wannan shine abun da gani akan books dinta....
Alhaji Auwal (Baffa) haifaffen garin katsina ne daga karamar hukumar batsari, mahuta village, bafullatanin ruga ne duk en uwanshi a can suke, mahaifiyarsa Inna a can take zaune don haka sau da yawa yana daukar family dinsa sukai masu ziyara, yana little brother guda daya amma he is no more sai d'an da ya bari wanda yake zaune a wurin Inna, she refuse to lived with them kuma taki bar mashi yaron, d'an kasuwa ne wanda ya shahara sosai a kasashen duniya shiyasa ba yawan zama a Nigeria, matarshi auranshi daya Alhaji Asmau bata da damuwa, she is really the meaning of kind, yaran suna kiranta da Ummy, both ita da baffa Fulani ne farare sol saidai yaran baki dashi carbon copy dinshi ne, Amal da Al'ameen suna matuk'ar kama fiye da tunani....

Wacece Amatullah?

Amal tace, "Saboda kayi kyauta da abuna shine kake tunanin biyana, thou ban so...".
Al'ameen ya kalleta yayinda murmushi ya sub'uce mashi yace,
"Kyauta zanyi maki... Uhmm har da mota zan siya maki mai zafi... Amma idan baki so shikenan kudina sun huta...".
Amal ta buge mashi baki tace,
"Bakin ciki zakayi min don kaga zanyi mota... Alright then ina so...".
Al'ameen yayi k'ara yace,
"Aauch... Baby akwai zafi...".
"Waye baby?".
"Amalulu...".
"Kaga ka daina b'ata min suna...".
"Alright then, na daina, bye, na wuce...".
"Safe trip... Aallah ya kiyaye hanya... Aallah ya tsare... Aallah ya kaika lafiya ya dawo da kai lafiya...".
Al'ameen yayi hugging dinta yayinda smile yayi escaping akan lips dinsa yace,
"Aameen...".
Amal ta zaro idanu tana tuno abun data gani a bathroom, (ni kuwa nace maganin wanda ba ya salaam kenan 😂)....
Al'ameen yayi breaking hug din tareda mata waving hand ya shige mota direba yayi mata key suka wuce....
Amal tayi tsaye totally speechless tana wani tunani, tayi firgigit ta juya ta koma ciki ta wuce bedroom ta fad'a akan gado bacci ne yayi awon gaba da ita, ta rungume pillow tana murmushi, can farka zaro idanu tayi tana kallon jikinta, tayi firgigit tareda zabura tana bin dakin da kallo, da sauri ta mik'e ta wuce bathroom.....

On Friday

Bashi da damuwa kamar yadda kaima baka da damuwa...".

"Amal... Are you hearing me...".

"Ina maki fatan Alkhairi a rayuwarki...".

On Thursday
Bayan Amal ta dawo daga school ta wuce sama ta rage kayan dake jikinta tayi wanka tareda shiryawa cikin wata breathtaking gown Indian design, ta sauko kasa ta wuce dinning table ta tsakuri abinci sannan ta mik'e ta wuce sama ta fad'a bathroom tayi brush ta fito tayi rolling veil garim sauri ta manta wayoyinta akan bedside drawer saida ta wuce gidan Hajiya inda ta tadda Amatullah da Hanan, saida suka soma hira tana kokarin duba abu a wayarta ta tuna na manta da ita a gida, ta dafe goshi sannan tace,

"Ke dai fad'i gaskiya, kode kode, kodai har da mukullin motarki kika manta, sabon shiga, how knows ko da ita zaki rink'a zuwa gidan Hajiyar... Aahhh silly girl... Villager...".

"Amal babu magana ne...".

Al'ameen yace, "Yeah...".
Ahmad yace, "Then na gani ina so, da fatan zaku bani...".
Amal ce ta sauko daga stairs, Al'ameen yace,
"Amal zaki bani aron motarki kinga tafi wadda nike hawa...".
Amal tace, "No...".
Al'ameen yace, "Alright then nima zan rama ne idan na sawo sabuwar mota kirar Ferrari Gt...".
Amal tace, "Alright then mu gani a kasa wai an cewa bestie ana biki gidansu tace amma cikina bai nuna min en kajin nan da zabbi...".
Har Ahmad saida yayi dariya, Amal tace,
"Am leaving... Huh... See you... Bye...".
Ta juya ta fice tana taking steps majestically wanda yanayinta ne haka....
Da daddare Al'ameen ne zaune a parlour suna hira, Amal ce ta sauko stairs ta zauna kusa dashi, tace,
"Yaa Al'ameen...".
Al'ameen ya murtuke fuska tareda d'aga mata hannu ✋ sannan yace,
"You don't have to say nothing, today I have seen your true colors...".
Amal ta dubeshi da mamaki sannan tace,
"Mey nayi kuma?".

Amal ta ware idanu yayinda Al'ameen ya fashe da dariya, Amal tace,
"No...".

SOME DAYS LATER

And don't you dare talk to her, bance kayi mata magana ba, ko kallon banza kadda kayi mata, akul, ashe ma tafi karfina idan har sai ka shigar min, ni da aikowa stimulus ni zan bata respond, watakila idan tana cin kasa by then zata kiyayi ta suri, aikin banza, aikin wofi... Ka daina bani hakuri, have you done something wrong, you didn't do nothing wrong, ba kayi min laifi ba wadda ta tsokanoni itace zata gane bata da wayo...".
Saidai ji yayi tayi hanging call din....
Kiran sallar magrib aka soma, Umma ta mik'e tace,
"Hanan ku tashi kuje kuyi sallah... Soffiyya idan kin gama cin abincin ki hau sama kiyi sallah... Shureim kaje waje ka kira en uwanka kuyi alwalla kubi yayanku masjid... Suma su Nasreem suyi a gida...".
Sannan ta wuce sama, Amal tace,
"Besty ku shirya gobe zamu wuce gidansu Aseey, dole ne in koya mata hankali...".
Suka mik'e suka haye stairs....
*******
Washe gari da misalin karfe sha daya na safe, Amal ce ta shirya cikin wasu riga da skirt na atamfa, tasha makeup tareda ture kallabin nan irin no respect, ta yafa veil akan shoulder, ta zura takalma flat, saida ta lek'a tayi ma Ummy salaam sannan ta fice ta shiga motarta tayi horn mai guard ya wangame mata get, saida ta fara daukar Amatullah sannan ta wuce ta dauki Hanan, suka wuce yayinda wani wicked smile yake escaping daga lips dinta, Hanan tana sanye ne da wani expensive less dinkin fitet gown tayi rolling veil while Amatullah take sanye da material dinkin bubu ta yafa katon veil mai kauri a saman kanta, kowace tayi light makeup irin no respect din nan, babu inda sukayi parking sai kofar kaura, a bakin wani katon mansion dake saman titi, Amal tayi horn mai guard ya wangame mata get ta danna motar ciki, tayi parking suka fito cike da k'asaita, Amal ce tayi knocking akan door, maid ce ta bud'e door ta gaidasu tana magana sukayi banza da ita suka shige ciki, sukayi tsaye a tsakiyar parlour suna k'are mashi kallo suna tab'e baki, Hajiyarsu Aseey tana daga baya tana karanta jarida amma basu lura da ita ba, Maid zatayi magana, Amal ta d'aga mata hannu sannan tace,
"Gidansu Aseey... Right? Thou wurinta muka zo, ki rakamu inda take...".
Maid ta wuce sama suka mara mata kallo, Hajiyarsu Aseey tabi en matan da kallo fuskarta dauke da mamaki....
Aseey tana zaune akan bed tayi tagumi, en matan ne suka shigo suna taking steps cike da k'asaita, suna karewa bedroom din kallo suna yatsine fuska....
Aseey ta d'ago da sauri, a razane ta mik'e tana kallonsu tana zaro idanu tana kallon kofa don bata son hajiyarta tasan halin da ake ciki, kallo daya za'ayi mata a fahimci a rud'e take, baki daya jikinta shaking yake, tana kafkafta idanu, en matan suka sheke da dariya, Amal tayi mata wani mugun kallo ta watsar sai kuma ta ciro faifan cd daga leather dinsa ta karasa gaban plasma dake a room din, ta bud'e DVD player ta saka, ta danna madanni ta kunna, bidiyo ne ya bayyana wanda Amatullah ta dauka lokacin da Aseey take kuka tana rokon Amal tabar mata Ashraim, Aseey ta k'ara rudewa tana kallon door tana raba idanu, Amatullah ta fashe da dariya sannan tace,
"Aahhh Amal da alama wannan yarinyar nan tayi hankali, ina ganin nan gaba ba hajiyarmu ba koda wani aka ce tayi ma rashin kunya ba zatayi ba...".
Hanan tayi wani shegen murmushi sannan tace,
"Aahhh watakila...".
Amal tayi taking steps tareda karasawa gaban Aseey, tayi mata wani kallo sannan tace,
"Aseey, last nayi maki warning baki dauka ba, shine kika bi Hajiyarmu kikayi mata rashin kunya, da alama yanzu kinyi hankali, idan kika sake kika k'ara shiga gonata then I will show you what am capable of doing, bari in gaya maki abun da baki ba sani ba, kin ganni nan bani son Ashraim, ya ganni ne kyauna da farina ya daukeshi ni kuma nike amfani da wannan damar nike wankar garan yadda ya kamata, idan na gama wankarshi na cika burina then I don't care auranshi zakiyi ko kuwa zuciya zakiyi kice saidai sonshi ya kasheki... Idan na gaya maki the other part of it, uhmm kanki bazai dauka ba...".
Sai kuma ta dafata sannan tace,
"Ohhh ni Amal rashin mutunci nazo inyi maki amma nike maki magana cikin lallami da lumana...".
Sai kuma ta murtuke fuska tareda pointing dinta, a fusace tace,
"Aseey ki fita daga rayuwata, babu ke babu Ashraim, koda kallonshi naji labarin kinyi uhmm you will pay for it, idan kin gadama gobe ki samu hajiya ki fad'i mata babu dadi then sai kinyi dana sanin sanina da kikayi, duk wani kishi da kikeji da hayaki da kanki yake ki ajiyeshi idan na wankeshi na gama na watsar dashi a dustbin sai kiyi da budurwar da zaiyi dating a gaba, idan kin gadama kije ki fad'a mashi saidai bazai tab'a yarda ba, even a look is enough for a wise amma ke na b'ata precious time dina nayi maki lalama sannan nayi maki warning, da fatan kin dauka, idan kunne yaji jiki ya tsira other wise jiki magayi... Babes let go...".

Page. 16


Amal, Amatullah da Hanan suka fice daga room suka sauko kasa suna yatsine fuska suka wuce compound, Hajiyarsu Aseey ta bisu da kallo sannan ta mik'e ta wuce sama....
Amal tana driving tana sakin wani wicked smile, can tace,
"Wannan Aseey din ta jawowa Ashraim dole ne in k'ara wankarshi, besty ku shirya gobe zamu shopping kuma dolene kowace ta dauki kaya masu tsada sosai shi kuma dole ya biya...".
Sukayi shewa, Amal tace,
"Ta b'ata min rai saboda shi don haka dole pocket dinshi ya girgiza...".
Kai tsaye suka wuce gidan Hajiya, suna bata labari suna dariya....
Da daddare,
Amal ta gama komai na shirin bacci har da addu'o'i ta kwanta akan bed tareda lumshe idanu, iPad dinta ce tayi ringing ta jawota tareda picking ta kanga a kunnenta tayi shiru, daga daya bangaren Ashraim yace,
"Amal...".
Saida tayi jim sannan tace,
"Yeah... Am hearing you...".
Yace, "Gobe zanzo...".
Tayi shiru sai kuma tace,
"A'a kayi zamanka, ni da kawayena zamuje shopping goben...".
Yace, "Can I join...".
Amal kanta tsaye tace,
"Why not idan kana so, saidai kasan it is impossible muje shopping tare sannan in rik'e trolley ina ja kaima ka rik'e trolley kana ja, saidai ni daya inyi kana jaye da trolley ina daukar abun da raina yake so ina sakawa...".
Da sauri yace,
"Sure, you wish is command, fad'i kawai zakiyi inyi...".
Amal tace, "Alright then...".
Saidai ji yayi tayi hanging call din, a bangarenta tana hanging call din ta kashe wayar baki daya, ta ajiyeta tareda lumshe idanu babu b'ata lokaci bacci yayi awon gaba da ita....
Washe gari,
Bayan Amal ta shirya ta wuce gidan Hajiya ta dauki Amatullah da Hanan suka wuce, tana driving tana wani wicked smile tana kad'a kai, a wani katon shopping mall tayi parking, Ashraim dake cikin motarsa ya fito tareda taking steps ya bud'e bangaren da take ta fito yana mata magana kasa-kasa tana murmushi, Amatullah da Hanan suka fito suka gaisa dashi, suka shige ciki, Ashraim ya zaro trolley tana taking steps yana turowa yana daga gefenta, tana daukar expensive abubuwa tana sakawa a ciki, wata expensive wristwatch ta dauka ta nuna a hannunshi tace,
"Tayi kyau?".
Ashraim yayi nodding head thinking that shi ta daukarmawa, unexpectedly yaji tace,
"Sure zatayi suiting Yaa Al'ameen, irinsu yake sakawa...".
A take yanayin fuskarsa ya sauya sai kuma yayi sauri yayi murmushi, da gayya ta rink'a daukar abubuwa sannan tace Al'ameen ta daukarmawa hakanan kuma bata fasa jidar expensive abubuwa, a counter suka hadu dasu Amatullah, aka yi bill ya biya, tabbas sun dauki kaya na makudan kudade, aka saka masu a manya-manyan leather aka wuce masu dasu mota, Ashraim ya raka Amal har mota, ya bud'e mata driver's seat ta shiga ta juyo, Ashraim ya dafa murfin motan yana mata magana kasa-kasa tana rolling idanu kawai, Ashraim yace,
"Sai nazo kenan?".
Amal tayi jim sannan tace,
"When?".
Ashraim yace, "Today...".
Amal tace, "Yau kam na gaji, gobe akwai abun da zanyi, the following day zamu fita da kawayena saidai the following day...".
Cike da damuwa Ashraim yace,
"Amal kiyi hakuri mana...".
Amal ta k'awar da kai sannan tace,
"Have you done something wrong, fad'a min abun da kayi... As I told ni ba kayi min laifi ba kuma wadda tayi min na sameta har inda take na nuna mata kuranta, thou you don't have to say Amal *BA NI DA LAIFI* kaga na wuce tun kafin ka b'ata min rai, am leaving...".
Ashraim yayi mata waving hand sannan ya matsa tareda rufo murfin motan, tayi reverse ta fice da motan, cike da damuwa Ashraim ya wuce motarsa ya jata ya koma inda ya fito....
Amal tayi wani wicked smile sannan tace,
"Shima little brother din anja mashi, gobe zamuje shopping kuma dole ya biya kudin, shima wankarshi zanyi...".
Sukayi shewa, saida ta saukesu sannan tayi gaba ta kama wata hanya, saida ta kyautar da shopping din da tayi sannan ta koma gida, throughout washe gari babu inda ta fita the same abun da ya faru kenan the following day, da daddare tana kwance tana kallo akan system, tayi picking call din Ashraif saida tayi jim sannan tace,
"Hello... Normal... Aahhh na gode... Nikam na gode... Yeah na gode... Gobe kuma?... Uhmmm gaskiya gobe bani nan, ni da kawayena zamuje shopping, thou kadda kazo bani nan... Yeah mi zai hana idan kana free... Alright then, sai mun hadu a can... Uhmmm inda ka fara ganina... Alright then... Bye... Na gode... Aahhh...".
Ta cire wayar daga kunnanta tareda hanging call din....
Washe gari,
Amal ta shirya ta dauki Hanan da Amatullah a motarta suka wuce shopping mall, kusan a tare motan Amal da motan Ashraif suka isa katon shopping mall din, ya fito tareda karasowa ya bud'e bangaren da take ta fito, Amatullah da Hanan suka fito suka gaisa dashi, suka wuce ciki, shima expensive abubuwa ta dauka saidai shi bata mashi abu da gayya ba, bayan ya biya bill na hug money aka saka kayan a manya-manyan leathers aka wuce masu dasu mota, tana ciki ya sunkuyo da kai yace,
"Amina sai yaushe kenan?".
Amal tayi far da idanu sannan tace,
"Sai kazo...".
Ashraif ya ware idanu sannan yace,
"When?".
Tace, "Today mana, ai dama kace min zakaje nace no gudun kadda kaje bani nan, yanzu kuma kasan lokacin da zaka sameni amma idan na huta...".
Ashraif yayi mata waving hand yace,
"Alright then sai nazo...".
Tace, "Alright then...".
Ta matsa tareda rufe murfin motan taja motar ta wuce sannan ya wuce da motarsa yana murmushi ya shiga yayi mata key ya koma inda ya fito....
Saida Amal ta saukesu Amatullah da Hanan a unguwarsu sannan tayi gaba, kamar wancen karon wannan karon ma saida ta kyautar da shopping dinta sannan ta koma gida....
********
Three days later,
Ashraim da Ashraif ne zaune suna hira, Ashraif yace,
"Buddy har yanzu busy ya hana zaje dani inga baby dinka, nuna min hotonta duk ranar da muke free baki daya sai kaini mu gaisa".
Ashraim yace, "Alright then, nima inga hoton babie dinka, let me see wadda tafi kyau...".
A tare suka zaro waya suka lalubo hotonta sannan suka mik'awa juna wayoyin, a tare suka d'ago da sauri suka kalli juna, idanu a waje, a sanadiyyar hoton wacce suka gani, Ashraim yace,
"Amal...".
Ashraif yace, "Amina...".
"Itace budurwata...".
"Nima itace budurwata...".
"Lokacin da na ganta a shopping mall tana tsaye ne a wurin perfumes...".
"Nima haka...".
"Tana da k'awaye guda biyu...".
"Yeah, farar buzuwa mai saka niqab da kuma wata baka mai hanci haka...".
A tare suka soma sweating, the more suke tattaunawa akan Amal the more suke fahimtar yadda ta raina masu hankali, tayi wasa da hankalinsu....
*******
Da marece,
Ashraif ne zaune a garden, a hankali Amal ta fito tana taking steps majestically tana murmushi, Ashraif ji yake kamar ya jawota yayi ta dukanta, haushinta yake ji, tana soyayya dashi sannan at the same time tana soyayya da brother dinshi wanda yafi so a rayuwarshi, wani mugun haushinta yake ji but he somehow manage to hide it, a hankali ta karaso ta zauna opposite dashi tana wani far da idanu, ji yake kamar yasa kafa yayi ball da ita, daurewa yayi yai murmushi yace,
"Amal...".
Amal ta dubeshi da sauri yayinda ya tsareta da idanu, yace,
"Yeah besties dinki naji suna kiranki da hakan...".
Amal tayi rolling idanu sannan tace,
"Hakane, sunana Amina A Mahuta amma kowa da kowa da Amal yake kirana...".
Ashraif daurewa kawai yake yana janta da hira amma ji yake kamar yayi ball da ita yaci gaba da dukanta yana takata yana ball da ita, can Amal tayi picking call, Ashraim ne, jim tayi sannan tace,
"On your way kuma?... Dama ina son ganinka... Alright then sai kazo... Alright then...".
Sannan tayi hanging call din, wani wicked smile ne yayi escaping akan lips dinta, cikin ranta tace,
"Aahhh wow ranar da na jima ina jiran zuwanta finally tazo, zan rabu da kaya, da alama yau ce ranar da na dade ina jira wadda zata zo in wankeku soso da sabulu, zaku gane kuranku...".
Amma a zahiri sai tayi dariya, Ashraif yace,
"Ya dai...".
Amal tace, "Wani abu na tuno...".
Ashraif mamaki take bashi, asirinta yana gab da tonuwa amma bata nuna alamar fargaba ba, can wayarshi tayi ringing yayi picking yayi jim sannan yace,
"Wa alaikassalaam... Emergency kuma?... Now... Alright then I will be on my way... Alright then...".
Hanging call din yayi sai kuma ya kalleta, ta marairaice idanu tace,
"Please kadda ka tafi...".
Ji yayi kamar yayi ball da it, yayi murmushi kawai tareda mata waving hand ya mik'e ya juya yayi taking steps ya shige motarsa ya jata ya fice daga gidan, Amal tayi kwafa tace,
"Aikin banza, aikin wofi, an b'ata min budget, I was thinking it is over daga yau amma yanzu an maida min hannun agogo baya, koda yake babu komai ai dole ranar zata zo, ranar da zasu gane gaskiya in wankesu soso da sabulu...".
Call ne tayi picking saida tayi jim sannan tace,
"Amma kace min kana akan hanya... Alright then... Alright then...".
Sai kuma ta cire wayar daga kunnanta ta tab'e baki tareda mikewa ta wuce ciki...
Washe gari da marece,
Al'ameen ne zaune a balcony ta sama yana aiki akan system amma lokaci zuwa lokaci yana dagowa ya kalli Amal da Ashraif dake tsaye a compound din gidan kusa da motar da Ashraif din yazo da ita, can mai guard ya wangame get wata expensive mota ta danno kai, aka yi parking dinta, Ashraim ne ya fito yayi taking steps har inda suke ya tsaya a gefen Ashraif, Al'ameen ya ajiye aikin da yake ya zuba masu idanu yana kallon abun da zai faru, Amal tayi wicked smile, kanta tsaye tace,
"Ashraif kaga brother dinka yazo, thou zaka wuce ne ko kuma tsayawa zaiyi gefe mu gama sannan tou zabi yana hannunka...".
Ashraif ya daka mata tsawa sannan yace,
"Hey... Amal, ke kam baki da kunya ne, despite dukkanin abun da kikayi amma shine kike wannan maganar banzar, kinji kunya...".
Amal ta daka mashi tsawa sannan tace,
"How dare, how dare you shout at me, erka ce ni ko kuma kanwarka, kunya da kake magana a kai, ai jin kunyar mara kunya asara ne, dukkaninku babu mai kunya a cikinku, wato koda kunya babu wai ni zaku titsiye, thou impossible, baru kuji abun da baku sani ba, komai da nayi maku nayi shi ne on purpose domin wannan ranar tazo in wankeku soso da sabulu, aikin banza aikin wofi, kun ganni kyauna da farina ya daukeku ni kuma nayi amfani dasu na wankeku na wanki kudi saidai bana tare da talauci ku dubi gidan da nike a ciki...".
Ashraim yace, "Amal kinyi mamu adalci kenan...".
Amal tayi mashi wani kallo idanunta a tsaitsaye tace,
"Kadda ka k'ara katseni barni in gama, ai ranar da muka fara haduwa barinku nayi kukayi don haka nima ku saurara min, take a look at katon mansion din nan, duk wanda yasan zabuwa thou da zanenta ya santa ballantana ace tattoo tayi, ita kuma barbela tun talo-talo take da haskenta ballantana ace bleaching tayi ko kuma ace wanka da sabulu ne ya saka tayi haske, en rainin sense kawai, kai ka fara fara ganina wato screen din iPhone 8 dina ya samu damuwa dakyar idan zata dannu kai kuma ka maidani barauniya, barauniya mana, maganarka hakan take nufi, thou kaga wayarka a hannuna kuma ba kai bane ka bani, ganinta kayi a hannuna ina duba abun da na ajiye, idan baku da hankali ni nayi maku, idan baku da manner yanzu kun nemo, baki dayanku garorina ne, kai dayan garana, kai gudan garana, da wadannan sunayen nayi saving numbers dinku, hope ka gani, kaima duba ka gani, nayi amfani da kyauna na wankeku na tara kudi daku, da kanku kuka bani cikin ruwan sanyi, dubeku babu ilimin addini sai tarin ilimin boko, kun tara kudi babu kyauta babu sadaqa saidai ku kashewa en mata, thou ni na wanki kudin kuma orphanage home zan kaisu, dubeku baku ajiye komai ba sai shegen son farar mace mai kyau thou suma wadanda ba fararen ba Aallah ne ya haliccesu... Shopping da kukayi min kyautar da kayan nayi... Kai har da bani leather inje in ba Momcy thou mai aiki na kyautar masu, kai kuma mai guard na kyautar masu, garori kawai...".
Ashraif ya daka mata tsawa tareda d'aga hannu zai wanka mata mari, da sauri Ashraim ya tare hannunsa, Amal idanunta a tsaitsaye cike da tsiwa tace,
"Kadda ka kuskura hannunka ya tab'a jikina, ka kafeni da idanu thou dukeni ka gani, idan kana tunanin idan ka dokeni ka doki banza kaci bulus thou ka sauya tunani, don kuwa a gidan nan muna da saurayi kamarka, ina da brother wanda kana dukana zai rama min, yana da karfi kamar yadda kakeji da karfi, duba saman bene ka gani...".
A tare suka d'aga kai suka kalli Al'ameen dake saman bene yana kallonsu, suka kalleshi ya kallesu sai kuma ya ware idanu ya fashe da dariya, suka maido dubansu a kanta, Amal tayi masu wani mugun kallo idanunta a tsaitsaye tace,
"You fool me and I fool you, two minus two equal to zero, amma kun d'an bani tausayi kadan don haka kuyi shawara a tsakaninku watakila daga baya in sauya shawara inyi soyayya da daya daga cikinku, ku tattara wadannan tarikitan motocin naku ku b'ace min daga gani or else you will see what am capable of doing, thou the choice is not mine but rather yours, hey you better get out of my site, I don't wanna see you any more, kun ganni nan na zame maku nan gani nan bari sadakin bazawara ai koda na budurwa ne yafi karfin wasu...".
Sai kuma ta ware hannuwa tareda masu wani kallo idanunta a tsaitsaye sannan ta juya tana taking steps kamar hawainiya, suna tsaye suna kallonta totally speechless har ta shiga ciki, a sanyaye suka juya kowane ya shige motarsa, suka fice....
Amal tana shiga parlour taci karo da Al'ameen, wani kallo yayi mata, da sauri tayi baya tana ware idanu a razane, tako daya biyu ya isa inda take ya kalleta yaga yadda ta razana, ji tayi ya fashe da dariya sannan yace,
"Bravo alaiki...".
Yana kallonta yana dariya yayi taking steps ya zauna akan kujera yana cigaba da dariya har da rik'e ciki....
Amal ta bishi da kallo cike da mamaki sannan tayi taking steps ta haye sama....
Da daddare,
Ashraif ne zaune a saman gado yayi zuru yana jin kamar son Amal zai kasheshi, on the other hand Ashraim ne tsaye a balcony ta daga sama yayi zuru yana jin zuciyarshi tana mashi zafi, yana matuk'ar son Amal....
Washe gari,
Ashraim da Ashraif ne, da karfi Ashraif yace,
"Babu inda zani, I will go no where, kana sonta bazan iya auranta ba, you love her more than I do, ka fini sonta, Idan kuma ba zaka aureta ba, shikenan mun rasa baki daya, tana da matuk'ar kyau saidai kyan d'an maciji ne, tana da kyau babu kyan hali...".
Ashraim ya girgiza kai sannan yace,
"A'a ka sani na sani kana son Amal, ka fini sonta sannan tana da kyan hali, laifi mukayi mata ta rama...".
Ashraif yace, "Please mu bar maganar nan, nace maka bazan iya ba...".
Ashraim ya taso mashi tareda daka mashi tsawa yace,
"Nace maka sai kaje, kaine ka fara ganinta...".
Ashraif yace, "Ka rabu dani da maganar yarinyar nan... I don't love her any more...".
Ashraim yace, "Karya kake kuma dole inyi maka maganarta saboda kana sonta don haka ka wuce kaje wurinta...".
Ashraif yace, "Nace maka ba zanje ba, I love her and you really do, na bar maka ita...".
Fad'a suke sosai wanda a tarihin rayuwarsu basu tab'a samun sab'ani ba, Ashraim ranshi ne ya soma b'aci, ya daka mashi tsawa sannan yace,
"Hey! Now you have to decide kodai kaje wurin Amal ko kuma babu ni babu kai, I mean am not your brother any more...".

Hanan tace, "Wai ina hurrey?".

"Don't tell me wani abun ta kara yi?".

Hanan tace, "Ku dai ku bari zan baku labari...".

"Alright then my dear little angel...".

Before tace wani abu yayi hanging call din, wurin karfe sha biyu ta shiga kitchen ta had'a masu lunch taci nata ta jera nashi a dinning table, ta koma sama already tayi sallah don haka ta tsala wanka tareda cancara kwalliya ta saka wasu riga da skirt English wears amma skirt din dogo ne, ta tamke kanta da veil, ta sauko ta hakimce a parlour....
Da smile kwance akan fuskarsa ya shigo ya ware hannuwa ta ruga da gudu ta fad'a jikinsa yayi sama da ita yana jujjuyawa da ita suna dariya, ya sauketa tace,
"Am really sorry...".
Al'ameen yace, "You don't have to apologize, abun da kikayi min shine na rama kiji idan akwai d'ad'i... Yanzu nima uhmm...".
Amal ta turo baki, ya dungure mata goshi yace,
"Kilbabba... Sai ince na fasa...".
Yayi sama da ita ya wuce sama, ya jima sosai sannan ya sauko shi daya ya wuce dinning table....
Da daddare yana matsawa kusa da ita, ta matsa tace,
"Am feeling sleepy...".
Al'ameen ya d'aure fuska saidai bai k'ara bi ta kanta ba, ya murgina daya bangaren, ta ware idanu kamar wadda ta tuno wani abu sannan ta murgina ta shige jikinsa yayi banza da ita, tace,
"Kayi hakuri... Bazan k'ara ba...".
Yace, "Better...".
Sai kuma ya soma mata cakulkulo suna dariya....
"Kamota nan... Kamota nan... Kamota nan...".
Tana dariya tana tureshi, can yayi hugging dinta very tight (Ni dai guduwa nayi, babu ruwana, besty ta hanani fad'a 🙊🙈)....
**********
Cikin ikon Aallah, Amal ta samu admission Medicine and Surgery, Baffa ya aiko mata da wata motoci guda biyu Benz da wata expensive Jeep, dasu take zuwa makaranta, wasu lokuttan yana rigata dawowa, a week end tare suke shiga kitchen, ta daina tsoronsa yanzu, sun koma kamar yadda suke a gida, shiga jikinsa take babu ruwanta, Hajiya ta kammala degree dinta, bautar kasa take a birnin tarayya Abuja, daman a nan mijinta yake aiki....
*********
SOME WEEKS LATER
Amal ce tareda wata yarinya ne suka fito daga lectures, long hijab ne a jikinsu baki daya, sunanta surayya, Amal ce ta durkushe tana kwarara amai, Surayya ta duk'e tareda kamata tana mata sannu, ta zaro battle water ta mik'a mata ta kuskure bakinta tareda wanke fuskarta, ta rakata har wurin motarta tana mata sannu, tace,
"Mahuta are you sure you can drive?".
Amal tace, "Kadda ki damu zan iya, na rasa abun dake damuna sai in rink'a jina very weak, yawan amai, kasala, ban so ne ya shiga damuwa shiyasa bana nunawa...".
Surayya tace, "I guess something...".
Amal tace, "What?".
Surayya tace, "A'a bazan fad'a maki ba, kadda in saka maki rai ya kasance ba haka bane... Bye... See you... Aallah ya kawo sauki...".
Amal tace, "Aameen...".
Sannan taja motan ta wuce gida....
Al'ameen ne zaune a balcony, yana karanta news paper, Amal tayi parking motarta ta fito tana tafiya kamar iska zai kayar da ita, jiri ne yake dibarta tayi luu, yayi sauri ya sareta saidai bud'e idanu tayi ta ganta akan gadon asibiti, har drop aka saka mata, Al'ameen da murnarsa ya nuna mata result tana da ciki d'an wata daya, yayi hugging dinta yana jera mata addu'a, tun a mota take kuka har suka isa gida, he thought kukan murna ne, ya wuce bathroom yayi wanka ya fito ya shirya cikin suit zai wuce wurin aiki, night duty gareshi, ya duk'a tareda mata peck a goshi yace,
"Am going... Stop crying... I love you...".
Amal tasa kuka tace,
"I will not... Na tashi ina kallonka a brotherna, duk wanda ya ganni da cikin nan yasan yadda aka yi, muje a cireshi...".
Al'ameen ya ware idanu sai kuma ya shafi fuskarta da smile akan fuskarsa yace,
"Silly girl... Abincinki yana akan dinning table, kadda ki shiga kitchen, ki kula mamu da little angel dinmu...".
Sai kuma yayi mata waving hand ya fice, saida ta raba dare tana kuka da taga yunwa tana neman tayi mata illa ta sauka kasa taci abinci, ta koma sama, bacci ne yayi awon gaba da ita, dakyar ta lallab'a tayi sallar Subh ta koma bacci, bacci ne sosai a idanunta, tana bacci Al'ameen ya dawo, bai tayar da ita ba, bacci ne a idanunsa sosai, ya rage kayan dake jikinsa ya wuce bathroom yayi wanka ya fito ya shirya cikin trouser tareda shirt mai wani irin neck, ya zauna gefen gado yana kurbar tea....
A hankali ta bud'e idanunta tayi jim tana kallon bayanshi sai kuma ta fashe da kuka sannan tace,
"Ka tashi muje asibiti a cire cikin nan, ni bazan iya da kayan kunyar nan ba...".
Al'ameen ya ware idanu yana kallonta, da mamaki yace,
"Amal are you serious? Da alama yau da rigima kika tashi, am feeling sleepy, ki barni inyi bacci, breakfast dinki yana akan dinning table, ya mik'e ya shiga bathroom ta bishi tana mashi kuka yayi brush tareda dauro alwalla ya fito tana biye dashi tana mashi kuka, ya kwanta akan bed tareda jawo blanket ya rufe jikinsa, yace,
"Amal, rigima, kije kiyi breakfast ki barni inyi bacci, bacci ne a idanuna sosai...".
Amal ta cika mashi kunne da kuka ta hanashi baccin da yake ji, ya tashi ya koma dayan bedroom din ta bishi can ta cika mashi kunne da kuka ta hanashi bacci, zuwa wannan lokacin abun ya fara b'ata mashi rai kuma ba ya son yayi mata ihu, ya sauko daga saman gadon ya fice ya sauka kasa ya wuce study room yayi locking dinshi from inside, ya dafe kanshi wanda ya fara mashi ciwo, ya jawo wayarshi ya dannawa Abdulhakeem call, yana picking bayan sunyi exchanging greeting, yayi jim sannan yace,
"Amal ce taki kwantar da hankalinta, ciki ne da ita tunda taji ta tada hankalinta wai sai an cireshi, kunya takeji ta tashi tana daukata a brother dinta sannan a ganta da cikina, taqi tayi breakfast, night duty nayi na dawo da bacci a idanuna amma ta tusani gaba da kuka, ban son inyi mata ihu, ka kira kayi mata magana...".
Sai kuma yayi hanging call din, dukkanin wannan maganar da Al'ameen yayi akan kunnan Ummy ne, suna London ne, sun kai mashi ziyara ne, Ummy ta tashi ranta a b'ace ta mik'e ta wuce bedroom ta jawo wayarta tana dannawa Amal call amma kasancewar Amal bata kusa da wayar, bata picking....
Amal ta gaji da knocking door din ta wuce sama ta durkushe a gefen gado tana kuka, wayarta ce tayi ringing ta jawota tayi picking tareda kangawa a kunne, Ummy ta balbaleta da fad'a....
"Ashe baki da hankali, ki rasa wanda zaki tadawa hankali sai mijinta, thou idan kikayi sanadiyyar aurenki sai ki dawo mu rink'a had'a kugu, ni kuma a sakani a bakin duniya, diya guda daya amma na kasa tarbiyyarta, duk wanda ya zageni a kanki sai Aallah ya saka min tunda dai na baki tarbiyya yadda ya kamata, tunda dai baki jin magana sai kiyi abun da kike so...".
Amal ta fashe da kuka, zatayi magana taji kiran ya tsinke, ta dafe kai tareda fashewa da kuka shine ya had'ata da mahaifiyarta, baccin wahala ne yayi awon gaba da ita, saidai farkawa tayi ta ganta kwance a jikinsa a saman gado, ta zuba mashi idanu, idanunsa a lumshe suke, ta kawar da fuskarta zata duro daga saman gadon ya rik'e hannunta yace,
"Amal...".
Amal ta dubeshi idanunsa a lumshe suke, tace,
"Shine ka kira Ummy ka fad'a mata, shine ka had'ani da mahaifiyata...".
Da sauri ya bud'e idanunsa ya mik'e zaune, yace,
"Abdulhakeem na kira, ban kira Ummy ba...".
Sai kuma ya jawo wayarshi ya danna call yayi jim sannan yace,
"Abdulhakeem... Mey yasa ka fad'awa Ummy?... Na gane... Alright then...".
Sai kuma ya cire wayar daga kunnansa yace,
"Tana kusa ne... Kiyi hakuri...".
Amal ta kama hannunsa tace,
"Yaa Al'ameen kayi hakuri, bazan k'ara ba...".
Al'ameen yayi murmushi tareda jan dogon hancinta yace,
"Good girl...".
Saidai ji tayi yayi sama da ita ya wuce bathroom....
*******
A London
Amatullah da Abdullahi (sweety) ne suka fito daga mota suka wuce wani shop, suna sanye ne da kayan sanyi masu kauri, hannayensu mak'ale da na juna, wasu mata da miji ne en Nigeria sukayi crossing dinsu, matar ta dawo da baya tareda shan gabansu, Bilqesu ce k'anwar late Abbakhar, wani kallo tayi masu sannan tace,
"Amatullah lallai kina jin dadinki, kinyi sanadiyyar rayuwarshi, kinyi mamu sanadin mutuwarshi amma gani kike kamar kinyi free, kadda ki damu akwai kotun Aallah, wata shari'ar sai a lahira...".
Ta juya taja hannun mijinta suka wuce, Amatullah ta fashe da kuka ta juya da sauri ta koma wurin mota ta durkushe tana kuka kamar ranta zai fice daga gangar jikinta, Abdullahi (sweety) ya karaso ya durkusa ya kamata yana goge mata hawaye yana rarrashinta amma a banza, mik'ewa yayi ya b'alle murfin motan ya kamata ta shiga ya rufo sannan ya zagaya bangaren direba, yana driving tana kuka, ji take mutuwar Abbakhar ta dawo mata sabuwa, a bakin wani katon mansion yayi horn aka wangame mashi get ya shige ciki....
***********
Cikin ikon Aallah saida su Amal saida sukayi hutun session sannan ta santalo diyarta mace mai kama dasu sak, washe gari Al'ameen ya wuce da ita Katsina bisa umarnin Ummy....
Amal taga gata, ta samu kulawa, haihuwar tana da kwana hudu Amatullah ta duro Nigeria tareda Abdullahi (sweety) tareda niki-nikin kayan uwar jego da baby dinta, the following day Hajiya ta duro Katsina, Hanan dama tare da ita ake zaman jego, ranar suna baby taci suna Mariam sunan late mahaifiyar Amal amma suna kiranta da Adla, raguna manya-manya guda ukku aka yanka, ansha shagali da naman kaji, chicken ba'a magana (har bafullo tayi mugun ci ta koma gida tana zaryar toilet 😱🙊👉😂) Amal da baby dinta sun samu alkhairi sosai, washe gari Kausar suka koma suka sha soye, bayan suna Ummy ta mayar da Amal sashen Inna sai sunyi arba'in sannan zata koma Abuja, Al'ameen ya dauki hutu ya dawo Katsina, tare suke kula da little princess dinsu, bayan sunyi arba'in ta shirya taje Mahuta yawon arba'in ta samu alkhairi, Ummy ta zage sosai ta saka aka gyara Amal in and out, suka wuce Abuja....
SOME MONTHS LATER
Amal ce tsaye a kitchen da apro a jikinta tana preparing meal, Al'ameen ne ya lek'o tareda shiga sadaf-sadaf yayi hugging dinta daga baya tareda bata peck a wuya, smile ne yayi escaping akan lips dinta, ya turo kanshi gefen fuskarta ya k'ara bata peck, ta lumshe idanu tace,
"More and more...".
Yana mata peck tana dariya, da sauri ya saketa yayi baya yace,
"Kina da baki a parlour".
Amal ta ware idanu tareda nunashi da lidde, ya gumtse lips tareda danne dariya dake son kufce mashi, tace,
"Shine baka fad'a min ba, mun shanyasu a parlour, you are very stubborn...".
Al'ameen yace, "Kece kika ce in k'ara kuma bazan maki musu ba... Nima ina so...".
Amal tayi pointing dinshi da lidde ya fita yana dariya, itama dariya tayi tareda girgiza kai, ta kashe gas tareda cire apro dake jikinta ta goge hannuwanta ta fice, ihu tayi ganin Amatullah da Hanan ne, tareda Abdullahi (sweety) da Hassan, ta ruga da gudu tayi hugging besties dinta tana tsallen murna, Al'ameen yayi folding hannuwa yana kallonta yana murmushi, Abdullahi (sweety) yana zaune akan sofa yana kallonsu yana murmushi hakanan Hassan, Hassan yace,
"A hankali, a hankali, kadda ki b'arar da babynki...".
Amal ta ware idanu tace,
"Wow... Hanan ciki ne dake... OMG...".
Amal ta kawo masu drinks sannan taja Amatullah suka wuce kitchen, ta hana Hanan ta biyosu amma saida ta shiga kasancewar cikin ba ya wani bata wahala sosai, kowace ta saka afro suka zage suka had'a delicious Hausa food, suka jera akan dinning table suka ci suka sha suka sha hira suka wuce garden suka hotuna da little princess dinsu, little Adla, daga nan suka wuce gidan Hajiya wadda take fama da karamin ciki yana wahalar da ita, burinsu baki daya akan ta haihu su sha shagala, idan suka zauna maganar kenan, itama Hanan sosai suke tanadin haihuwarta amma na Hajiya daban ne, lallai wannan ciki zai samu gata, they live happy amma dole rayuwa tana zuwa ne with joy and happiness tareda kalubale....

ALHAMDULILLAH
THE END
A nan ne muka kawo karshen wannan novel named *en mata* da fatan kunyi enjoying dinsa, kuyi amfani da darussan dake ciki sannan ku watsar da kurakuren da kuka ci karo dasu, idan wani abun ya b'ata maku rai kuyi hakuri, you guys kuna raina, kirkirarren labari ne idan yayi daidai da rayuwarku arashi ne, wannan novel din baki daya sadaukarwa ne a gareki our beloved sister, small cute Hajja, Hajiya Fateema, you are amazing... Da fatan zakuyi azumi lafiya... Bye... One love... Ku sakani a addu'o'enku 🙏🙏🙏...
